=== SINK INVENTORY (Layer 3a / D4) — NON-TEST ===
Directive: D4   Layer: 3a (deterministic sink inventory)   Variant: NON-TEST
Objective: deterministic, 100%-recall enumeration of dangerous SINK call-sites across 19 CWE-mapped
categories over first-party, NON-TEST source. Consumed by Layer 3b (taint analysis). READ-ONLY:
this audit modifies no source files.
Repository: calcom-monorepo (blitzy-cal)
Generated-by: deterministic 'grep -rInE' sweeps (one per category), test-path-filtered, ANSI/CR-stripped,
sorted byte-stably via 'LC_ALL=C sort -t: -k1,1 -k2,2n'. Byte-identical on identical repository input.

Include extensions: .ts .tsx .js .jsx .mjs .cjs
exclude_dirs: node_modules .yarn .next dist build .turbo coverage .git
Test-file exclusion (recorded separately in sink-inventory-test.txt, NOT here): paths matching
  *.test.*  *.spec.*  *-test.*  *.e2e.*  *.test-suite.*  *.integration-test.*
  or under  __tests__/  /test/  /tests/  /e2e/
Active language dimension: JavaScript/TypeScript. Python/Go/Java sink columns are structurally empty
(N/A) for this codebase per AAP 0.8.2 — that emptiness is NOT a failure.
Line format per match:  <file>:<line>:<source snippet>
Per category below: a '--- NAME (CWE) ---' header, the 'pattern:' used, the sorted matching lines,
then 'count:' (number of matching lines) and 'files:' (number of unique files).
